### Q203. MHT CET 2021 (22 Sep Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

What is the molar mass of metal having density $8.57 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3}$ and edge length $3.3 Å$ ? (Packing efficiency $=68 \%$ )

**Options:**

- **(A)** $63 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~mol}^{-1}$
- **(B)** $93 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~mol}^{-1}$
- **(C)** $29 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~mol}^{-1}$
- **(D)** $39 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~mol}^{-1}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) $93 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~mol}^{-1}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

Packing efficiency $=68 \%$
BCC unit cell
$$
\begin{aligned}
& \mathrm{Z}=2, \mathrm{a}=3.3 Å \\
& =3.3 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm} \\
& \mathrm{~d}=8.57 \mathrm{~g} / \mathrm{cm}^3 \\
& \mathrm{~d}=\frac{\mathrm{Z} \cdot \mathrm{M}}{\mathrm{N}_{\mathrm{A}} \cdot \mathrm{a}^3} \\
& \mathrm{M}=\frac{\mathrm{d} \times \mathrm{N}_{\mathrm{A}} \cdot \mathrm{a}^3}{\mathrm{Z}}=\frac{8.57 \times 6.022 \times 10^{23} \times\left(3.3 \times 10^{-8}\right)^3}{2} \\
& \mathrm{M}=93 \mathrm{~g} / \mathrm{mol}
\end{aligned}
$$

</details>

---

### Q204. MHT CET 2021 (21 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `3`

What is the density of an element (At. mass $100 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~mol}^{-1}$ ) having BCC structure with edge length $400 \mathrm{pm}$ ?

**Options:**

- **(A)** $3.2 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3}$
- **(B)** $8.2 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3}$
- **(C)** $5.18 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3}$
- **(D)** $4.8 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) $5.18 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

$\begin{aligned} & \mathrm{a}=400 \mathrm{pm}=400 \times 10^{-12} \mathrm{~m} \\ & =400 \times 10^{-10} \mathrm{~cm} \\ & =4 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm} \\ & \mathrm{Z}=2(\text { for } \mathrm{BCC}) \\ & \mathrm{d}=\frac{\mathrm{Z} \times \mathrm{M}}{\mathrm{N}_{\mathrm{A}} \cdot \mathrm{a}^3}=\frac{2 \times 100}{6.022 \times 10^{23} \times\left(4 \times 10^{-8}\right)^3}=5.18 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3}\end{aligned}$

</details>

### Q205. MHT CET 2021 (21 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `3`

What is the total number of atoms in BCC crystal lattice having $1.8 \times 10^{20}$ unit cells?

**Options:**

- **(A)** $9.0 \times 10^{20}$
- **(B)** $1.8 \times 10^{20}$
- **(C)** $3.6 \times 10^{20}$
- **(D)** $7.2 \times 10^{20}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) $3.6 \times 10^{20}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

In one BCC crystal unit cell number of atoms $(Z)=2$
Total number of atoms $=\mathrm{Z} \times$ Number of unit cells
$$
\begin{aligned}
& =2 \times 1.8 \times 10^{20} \\
& =3.6 \times 10^{20}
\end{aligned}
$$

</details>

### Q206. MHT CET 2021 (21 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

What is the percentage of unoccupied volume in BCC structure?

**Options:**

- **(A)** $32 \%$
- **(B)** $74 \%$
- **(C)** $26 \%$
- **(D)** $68 \%$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) $32 \%$**

**Detailed Solution:**

Packing efficiency
$$
\begin{aligned}
& \text { P.E. }=\frac{z \times \frac{4}{3} \pi \mathrm{r}^3}{V}=\frac{2 \times \frac{4}{3} \times \pi\left(\frac{\sqrt{3} a}{4}\right)^3}{a^3}=\frac{\sqrt{3} \pi}{8}=0.68 \\
& {\left[\because z=2, r=\frac{\sqrt{3} a}{4}, V=a^3\right]}
\end{aligned}
$$
In B.C.C., $68 \%$ of total volume is occupied by atoms or ions.
And $100-68=32 \%$ of total volume is unoccupied.

</details>

---

### Q207. MHT CET 2021 (21 Sep Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

An element with simple cubic close structure has edge length of unit cell $3.86 Å$. What is the radius of atom?

**Options:**

- **(A)** $5.79 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}$
- **(B)** $1.93 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}$
- **(C)** $3.86 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}$
- **(D)** $2.43 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) $1.93 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

In simple cubic close structure
$$
\begin{aligned}
& \mathrm{a}=2 \mathrm{r} \\
& \mathrm{r}=\frac{\mathrm{a}}{2}=\frac{3.86}{2}=1.93 Å \\
& \mathrm{r}=1.93 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm} \\
& \left(1 \mathrm{~A}^{\circ}=10^{-10} \mathrm{~m}=10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}\right)
\end{aligned}
$$

</details>

### Q208. MHT CET 2021 (21 Sep Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

Which is the volume of unit cell of a metal (at. mass $25 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~mol}^{-1}$ ) having BCC structure and density $3 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3}$ ?

**Options:**

- **(A)** $3.64 \times 10^{-23} \mathrm{~cm}^3$
- **(B)** $1.56 \times 10^{-24} \mathrm{~cm}^3$
- **(C)** $2.76 \times 10^{-23} \mathrm{~cm}^3$
- **(D)** $1.88 \times 10^{-24} \mathrm{~cm}^3$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) $2.76 \times 10^{-23} \mathrm{~cm}^3$**

**Detailed Solution:**

$$
\begin{aligned}
& \text { Density }=\frac{\text { mass of atomsin unitcell }}{\text { Volume of unitcell }} \\
& \text { mass of atoms in unit cell }=\frac{\mathrm{Z} \times \mathrm{M}}{\mathrm{N}_{\mathrm{A}}}=\frac{2 \times 25}{6.023 \times 10^{23}} \\
& \text { Volume of unit cell }=\frac{2 \times 25}{6.02 \times 10^{23} \times 3}=2.76 \times 10^{-23} \mathrm{~cm}^3
\end{aligned}
$$

</details>

---

### Q209. MHT CET 2021 (21 Sep Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

What is the percentage efficiency of packing in BCC structure?

**Options:**

- **(A)** $32 \%$
- **(B)** $74 \%$
- **(C)** $26 \%$
- **(D)** $68 \%$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) $68 \%$**

**Detailed Solution:**

Packing efficiency:
$$
\begin{aligned}
& \text { P.E. }=\frac{\mathrm{z} \times \frac{4}{3} \pi \mathrm{r}^3}{V}=\frac{2 \times \frac{4}{3} \times \pi\left(\frac{\sqrt{3} \mathrm{a}}{4}\right)^3}{\mathrm{a}^3}=\frac{\sqrt{3} \mathrm{a}}{8}=0.68 \\
& {\left[\because \mathrm{z}=2, \mathrm{r}=\frac{\sqrt{3} \mathrm{a}}{4}, V=\mathrm{a}^3\right]}
\end{aligned}
$$
In B.C.C., $68 \%$ of total volume is occupied by atoms or ions.

</details>

### Q211. MHT CET 2021 (20 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

What is the number of unit cells present in $3.9 \mathrm{~g}$ of potassium if it crystallizes in BCC Structure?

**Options:**

- **(A)** $\frac{\mathrm{N}_{\mathrm{A}}}{10}$
- **(B)** $\mathrm{N}_{\mathrm{A}} \times 10$
- **(C)** $2 \mathrm{~N}_{\mathrm{A}}$
- **(D)** $\frac{\mathrm{N}_{\mathrm{A}}}{20}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) $\frac{\mathrm{N}_{\mathrm{A}}}{20}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

$\begin{aligned} & \text { Number of atoms }=\frac{\text { Mass }}{\text { Atomicmass }} \times \mathrm{N}_{\mathrm{A}}=\frac{3.9}{39} \times \mathrm{N}_{\mathrm{A}}=0.1 \mathrm{~N}_{\mathrm{A}} \\ & \text { In BCC unit cell, } \mathrm{n}=2 \\ & \therefore \text { Number of unit cells }=\frac{0.1 \mathrm{~N}_{\mathrm{A}}}{2}=\frac{\mathrm{N}_{\mathrm{A}}}{20}\end{aligned}$

</details>

### Q212. MHT CET 2021 (20 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

An element (molar mass 180) has BCC crystal structure with density $18 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3}$. What is the edge length of unit cell?

**Options:**

- **(A)** $\sqrt[3]{23.2} \times 10^{-24} \mathrm{~cm}$
- **(B)** $\sqrt[3]{12.6} \times 10^{-24} \mathrm{~cm}$
- **(C)** $\sqrt[3]{33.2} \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}$
- **(D)** $\sqrt[3]{22.6} \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) $\sqrt[3]{33.2} \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

$$
\mathrm{M}=180 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~mol}^{-1}, \rho=18 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3} \text {, }
$$
For BCC crystal, $\mathrm{z}=2, \mathrm{a}=$ ?
$$
\begin{aligned}
& \rho=\frac{M \times z}{a^3 \times N_A} \quad \therefore a^3=\frac{M \times z}{\rho \times N_A} \\
& \therefore a^3=\frac{180 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~mol}^{-1} \times 2 \text { atom }}{18 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3} \times 6.022 \times 10^{23} \text { atom mol }} \\
& \therefore a^3=33.2 \times 10^{-24} \mathrm{~cm}^3 \\
& \therefore a=\sqrt{33.2} \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}
\end{aligned}
$$

</details>

---

### Q213. MHT CET 2021 (20 Sep Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `3`

How many total voids are present in 1 mole of compound that forms hcp structure?

**Options:**

- **(A)** $1.806 \times 10^{24}$
- **(B)** $1.204 \times 10^{24}$
- **(C)** $3.011 \times 10^{23}$
- **(D)** $6.022 \times 10^{23}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) $1.806 \times 10^{24}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

In HCP structure
For each atom total three voids are present (one octahedral and two tetrahedral) Number of atoms in $1 \mathrm{~mol}$ of compound $=6.022 \times 10^{23}$

Total number of voids $=$ No. of atoms $\times 3$
$$
\begin{aligned}
& =6.022 \times 10^{23} \times 23 \\
& =18.06 \times 10^{23} \\
& =1.806 \times 10^{24}
\end{aligned}
$$

</details>

### Q214. MHT CET 2021 (20 Sep Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

What is molar mas of metal with BCC structure having density $10 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3}$ and edge length $200 \mathrm{pm}$ ?

**Options:**

- **(A)** $90.2 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~mol}^{-1}$
- **(B)** $21.1 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~mol}^{-1}$
- **(C)** $48.0 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~mol}^{-1}$
- **(D)** $60.5 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~mol}^{-1}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) $21.1 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~mol}^{-1}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

BCC structure,
$\mathrm{Z}=2$ (Number of atoms in one unit cell)
$$
\begin{aligned}
& \mathrm{d}=\frac{\mathrm{Z} \cdot \mathrm{M}}{\mathrm{N}_{\mathrm{A}} \cdot \mathrm{a}^3} \\
& \mathrm{a}=200 \mathrm{pm}=200 \times 10^{-10} \mathrm{~cm} \text { or } 2 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm} \\
& 10=\frac{2 \times \mathrm{M}}{6 \times 10^{23} \times\left(2 \times 10^{-8}\right)^3} \\
& 0=\frac{2 \times \mathrm{M}}{6 \times 10^{23} \times 8 \times 10^{-24}} \\
& \mathrm{M}=\frac{10 \times 6 \times 10^{23} \times 10^{-24} \times 8}{2} \\
& =24 \mathrm{~g} / \mathrm{mol}
\end{aligned}
$$

</details>

### Q218. MHT CET 2020 (19 Oct Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Which among the following crystal lattices occupies all of the cubic holes by cations?

**Options:**

- **(A)** $\mathrm{SrCl}_{2}$
- **(B)** $\mathrm{CaF}_{2}$
- **(C)** $\mathrm{CsCl}$
- **(D)** $\mathrm{UO}_{2}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) $\mathrm{CsCl}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

$\mathrm{CsCl}$
Cations inhabited all of the cubic holes in $\mathrm{CsCl}$ crystal lattices. A single atom in the cube's centre belongs to this unit cell, and the unit cell has only one body centre. As a result, the compound's formula is $\mathrm{CsCl}$, which stands for body-centred cubic cell. Particles are present in the corners of the cube, as well as one particle in the centre of the body, in this sort of cell.

### Q219. MHT CET 2020 (19 Oct Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

An element crystallises in fcc type of unit cell. The volume of one unit cell is $24.99 \times 10^{-24} \mathrm{~cm}^{3}$ and density of the element $7 \cdot 2 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3} .$ Calculate the number of unit cells in $36 \mathrm{~g}$ of pure sample of element?

**Options:**

- **(A)** $2 \cdot 0 \times 10^{23}$
- **(B)** $2 \cdot 0 \times 10^{21}$
- **(C)** $2 \cdot 0 \times 10^{24}$
- **(D)** $1 \cdot 25 \times 10^{21}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) $2 \cdot 0 \times 10^{23}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

(C)
Vol. of element $=\frac{\text { Mass }}{\text { Density }}=\frac{36 \mathrm{~g}}{7.2 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3}}=5 \mathrm{~cm}^{3}$
No. of unit cells in $36 \mathrm{~g}$ of pure sample of element $=\frac{\text { Total Vol. of element }}{\text { Vol. of one unit cell }}$
$=\frac{5 \mathrm{~cm}^{3}}{24.99 \times 10^{-24} \mathrm{~cm}^{3}}=2.0 \times 10^{23}$

</details>

### Q223. MHT CET 2020 (16 Oct Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

An element has a bcc structure with cell edge of $288 \mathrm{pm}$. The density of element is $7.2 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3}$. What is the atomic mass of an element?

**Options:**

- **(A)** $51.78$
- **(B)** $25.89$
- **(C)** $62.43$
- **(D)** $77.68$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) $51.78$**

**Detailed Solution:**

(D)
$\begin{array}{l}
\mathrm{a}=288 \mathrm{pm}=2.88 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm} \\
\therefore \mathrm{a}^{3}=\left(2.88 \times 10^{-8}\right)^{3} \mathrm{~cm}^{3}=2.39 \times 10^{-23} \mathrm{~cm}^{3} \\
\rho=7.2 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3}, \mathrm{n}=2(\text { for bcc cell }) \\
\therefore \mathrm{M}=\frac{\rho \times \mathrm{a}^{3} \times \mathrm{N}_{\mathrm{A}}}{\mathrm{n}}=\frac{7.2 \times 2.39 \times 10^{-23} \times 6.022 \times 10^{23}}{2}=51.81 \mathrm{~g}
\end{array}$

</details>

### Q224. MHT CET 2020 (16 Oct Shift 2)

**Options:**

- **(B)** $111.91 \mathrm{pm}$

- **(D)** $158.25 \mathrm{pm}$

<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) $158.25 \mathrm{pm}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

(B)
$\mathrm{a}=316.5 \mathrm{pm}$
For fcc unit cell, $r=\frac{a}{2 \sqrt{2}}$
$\therefore r=\frac{316.5 \mathrm{pm}}{2 \times 1.414}=111.91 \mathrm{pm}$

</details>

### Q225. MHT CET 2020 (16 Oct Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

An element crystallises in a bcc lattice with cell edge of $500 \mathrm{pm}$. The density of the element is $7.5 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3}$. How many atoms are present in $300 \mathrm{~g}$ of metal ?

**Options:**

- **(A)** $12 \cdot 8 \times 10^{23}$ atoms
- **(B)** $6 \cdot 4 \times 10^{23}$ atoms
- **(C)** $3 \cdot 2 \times 10^{23}$ atoms
- **(D)** $1 \cdot 6 \times 10^{23}$ atoms

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) $6 \cdot 4 \times 10^{23}$ atoms**

**Detailed Solution:**

(B)
$a=500 \mathrm{pm}=5 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}$
$\therefore \mathrm{a}^{3}=\left(5 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}\right)^{3}=125 \times 10^{-24} \mathrm{~cm}^{3}$
$\rho=7.5 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3}, \mathrm{~m}=300 \mathrm{~g}, \mathrm{n}=2($ for bcc cell $)$
$\rho=\frac{\mathrm{nM}}{\mathrm{a}^{3} \mathrm{~N}_{\mathrm{A}}} \quad \therefore \mathrm{M}=\frac{\rho \times \mathrm{a}^{3} \times \mathrm{N}_{\mathrm{A}}}{\mathrm{n}}$
$\therefore M=\frac{7.5 \times 125 \times 10^{-24} \times 6.022 \times 10^{23}}{2}=282.3 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~mol}^{-1}$
$282.3 \mathrm{~g}$ of metal contains $6.022 \times 10^{23}$ atoms
$\therefore 300 \mathrm{~g}$ of metal contains $\frac{6.022 \times 10^{23} \times 300}{282.3}=6.399 \times 10^{23}$ atoms $\approx 6.4 \times 10^{23}$ atoms

</details>

---

### Q226. MHT CET 2020 (16 Oct Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A metallic element crystallises in simple cubic lattice. If edge legth of the unit cell is $3 A^{\circ}$, with density $8 \mathrm{~g} / \mathrm{cc}$, what is the number of unit cells in $100 \mathrm{~g}$ of the metal? $($ Molar mass of metal $=108 \mathrm{~g} / \mathrm{mol}$ )

**Options:**

- **(A)** $1.33 \times 10^{20}$
- **(B)** $2 \times 10^{24}$
- **(C)** $2.7 \times 10^{22}$
- **(D)** $5 \times 10^{23}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) $5 \times 10^{23}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

$(\mathrm{B})$
$\mathrm{a}=3 Å=3 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}$
Volume of the unit cell $\left(\mathrm{a}^{3}\right)=\left(3 \times 10^{-8}\right)^{3} \mathrm{~cm}^{3}=27 \times 10^{-24} \mathrm{~cm}^{3}$
Mass of unit cell $=27 \times 10^{-24} \mathrm{~cm}^{3} \times 8 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3}$
$=216 \times 10^{-24} \mathrm{~g}$
$\begin{aligned} 216 \times 10^{-24} \mathrm{~g}=1 \text { unit cell } & \\ \therefore 108 \mathrm{~g}=& \frac{108}{216 \times 10^{-24}}=5 \times 10^{23} \text { unit cells. } \end{aligned}$

</details>

### Q228. MHT CET 2020 (16 Oct Shift 1)

A compound has fcc structure. If density of unit cell is $3 \cdot 4 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3}$, what is the edge length of unit cell? (Molar mass $=98.99$ )

### Q231. MHT CET 2020 (15 Oct Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

What is the volume of 1 mole of a crystalline solid having unit cell edge length $16 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}$, if it's unit cell contains 24 molecules?

**Options:**

- **(A)** $102 \cdot 7 \mathrm{~cm}^{3} \mathrm{~mol}^{-1}$
- **(B)** $404 \cdot 0 \mathrm{~cm}^{3} \mathrm{~mol}^{-1}$
- **(C)** $159 \cdot 3 \mathrm{~cm}^{3} \mathrm{~mol}^{-1}$
- **(D)** $142 \cdot 1 \mathrm{~cm}^{3} \mathrm{~mol}^{-1}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) $102 \cdot 7 \mathrm{~cm}^{3} \mathrm{~mol}^{-1}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

$16 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}$
$a=16 \times 10^{-8}$ cme unit cell $=a^{3}=\left(16 \times 10^{-8}\right)^{3}$
Volume of one unit cellat $=4096 \times 10^{-24} \mathrm{~cm}^{3}$
volume of 24 molecules $=4096 \times 10^{-24} \mathrm{~cm}^{3}$
$\therefore$ Volume of 1 molecule $=\frac{4096 \times 10^{-24}}{24}=170.67 \times 10^{-24} \mathrm{~cm}^{3}$
$\begin{aligned} \therefore \text { Volume of } 1 \text { mole of a crystalline solid } &=170.67 \times 10^{-24} \times 6.022 \times 10^{23} \\ &=1027.77 \times 10^{-1} \end{aligned}$

</details>

---

### Q232. MHT CET 2020 (15 Oct Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Sodium crystallises in bcc structure with radius $1 \cdot 86 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}$. Calculate the edge length of unit cell?

**Options:**

- **(A)** $4.29 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}$
- **(B)** $6 \cdot 20 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}$
- **(C)** $8 \cdot 05 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}$
- **(D)** $3 \cdot 72 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) $4.29 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

For bcc unit cell, $r=\frac{\sqrt{3} a}{4}$
$\therefore \quad \mathrm{a}=\frac{4 \mathrm{r}}{\sqrt{3}}=\frac{4 \times 1.86 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}}{1.732}=4.29 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}$

</details>

### Q233. MHT CET 2020 (15 Oct Shift 1)

### Q234. MHT CET 2020 (14 Oct Shift 2)

### Q235. MHT CET 2020 (14 Oct Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

An element crystallises in a fcc lattice with cell edge $250 \mathrm{pm}$. Calculate the density of an element
(at.mass $=90 \cdot 3$ )

**Options:**

- **(A)** $23 \cdot 12 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3}$
- **(B)** $19 \cdot 20 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3}$
- **(C)** $48 \cdot 40 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3}$
- **(D)** $38.40 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) $38.40 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

$\mathrm{a}=250 \mathrm{pm}=2.5 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}$
$\mathrm{M}=90.3 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~mol}^{-1}, \mathrm{n}=4($ for fcc cell $)$
$\rho=\frac{n \times M}{a^{3} \times N_{A}}=\frac{4 \times 90.3}{\left(2.5 \times 10^{-8}\right)^{3} \times 6.022 \times 10^{23}}$
$\therefore \rho=38.40 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3}$

</details>

---

### Q236. MHT CET 2020 (14 Oct Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

An element with density $2 \cdot 8 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3}$ forms fec unit cell having edge length $4 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}$. Calculate molar mass of the element.

**Options:**

- **(A)** $33 \cdot 0 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~mol}^{-1}$
- **(B)** $22 \cdot 0 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~mol}^{-1}$
- **(C)** $27 \cdot 0 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~mol}^{-1}$
- **(D)** $36 \cdot 0 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~mol}^{-1}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) $27 \cdot 0 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~mol}^{-1}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

$\rho=2.8 \mathrm{gcm}^{-3}, \quad \mathrm{a}=4 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}$,
$\mathrm{n}=4($ for fcc),$\quad \mathrm{M}=?$
$\rho=\frac{\mathrm{n} \times \mathrm{M}}{\mathrm{a}^{3} \times \mathrm{N}_{\mathrm{A}}} \quad \therefore \mathrm{M}=\frac{\rho \times \mathrm{a}^{3} \times \mathrm{N}_{\mathrm{A}}}{\mathrm{n}}$
$\therefore M=\frac{2.8 \times\left(4 \times 10^{-8}\right)^{3} \times 6.022 \times 10^{23}}{4}=26.97 \approx 27 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~mol}^{-1}$

</details>

### Q240. MHT CET 2020 (13 Oct Shift 2)

### Q241. MHT CET 2020 (13 Oct Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

An element crystallises bcc type of unit cell, the density and edge length of unit
cell is $4 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3}$ and 500 pm respectively. What is the atomic mass of an element?

**Options:**

- **(A)** $125 \cdot 5$
- **(B)** $100 \cdot 1$
- **(C)** $250 \cdot 0$
- **(D)** $150 \cdot 0$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) $150 \cdot 0$**

**Detailed Solution:**

In bcc, $\mathrm{n}=2, \rho=4 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3}$
$\mathrm{a}=500 \mathrm{pm}=500 \times 10^{-10} \mathrm{~cm}=5 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}$
$\therefore$ Volume of unit cell, $\mathrm{a}^{3}=\left(5 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}\right)^{3}=125 \times 10^{-24} \mathrm{~cm}^{3}$
Now, $\mathrm{M}=\frac{\rho \times \mathrm{a}^{3} \times \mathrm{N}_{\mathrm{A}}}{\mathrm{n}}=\frac{4 \times 125 \times 10^{-24} \times 6.022 \times 10^{23}}{2}=150.5 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~mol}^{-1}$

</details>

### Q244. MHT CET 2020 (13 Oct Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

How many number of unit cells are present in $100 \mathrm{~g}$ of an element with fcc crystal having density $10 \mathrm{~g} / \mathrm{cm}^{3}$ and edge length $100 \mathrm{pm}$ ?

**Options:**

- **(A)** $3 \times 10^{25}$
- **(B)** $2 \times 10^{25}$
- **(C)** $4 \times 10^{25}$
- **(D)** $1 \times 10^{25}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) $1 \times 10^{25}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

$\begin{aligned} \text { Vol. of unit cell } &=(100 \mathrm{pm})^{3}=\left(100 \times 10^{-10} \mathrm{~cm}\right)^{3} \\ &=10^{-24} \mathrm{~cm}^{3} \end{aligned}$
Vol. of $100 \mathrm{~g}$ of an element $=\frac{\text { Mass }}{\text { Density }}=\frac{100 \mathrm{~g}}{10 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3}}=10 \mathrm{~cm}^{3}$
No. of unit cells in $100 \mathrm{~g}$ of an element $=\frac{\text { Total volume }}{\text { Volume of one unit cell }}$
$=\frac{10 \mathrm{~cm}^{3}}{10^{-24} \mathrm{~cm}^{3}}=1 \times 10^{25}$ unit cells.

</details>

---

### Q245. MHT CET 2020 (12 Oct Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

What is the type of hole occupied if limiting value of $\frac{\mathrm{r}+}{\mathrm{r}-}$ is in the range of $0.225$ to $0.414 ?$

**Options:**

- **(A)** Octahedral
- **(B)** Cubic
- **(C)** Planar triangular
- **(D)** Tetrahedral

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) Tetrahedral**

**Detailed Solution:**

The radius ratio is the ratio of the radius (r) of the atom or ion which can exactly fit in the octahedral void formed by spheres of radius $R$. For trigonal voids, the radius ratio is $0.155$.
For tetrahedral voids, the radius ratio is $0.225$.
For octahedral voids, the radius ratio is $0.414$.
For cubic voids, the radius ratio is $0.732$.
Thus, the limiting radius ratio for tetrahedral structure is $0.225-0.414$.

</details>

### Q247. MHT CET 2020 (12 Oct Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

What is the mass of an fcc unit cell if mass of one atom of an element is $6 \times 10^{-23} \mathrm{~g}$ ?

**Options:**

- **(A)** $24 \times 10^{-22} \mathrm{~g}$
- **(B)** $4 \times 10^{-23} \mathrm{~g}$
- **(C)** $2 \cdot 4 \times 10^{-22} \mathrm{~g}$
- **(D)** $2 \cdot 4 \times 10^{-23} \mathrm{~g}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) $2 \cdot 4 \times 10^{-22} \mathrm{~g}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

In FCC, No. of atoms per unit cell $=4$
$\begin{aligned}
\text { Mass of unit cell } &=\text { Mass of } 4 \text { atoms }=4 \times 6 \times 10^{-23} \mathrm{~g} \\
&=24 \times 10^{-23} \mathrm{~g}=2.4 \times 10^{-22} \mathrm{~g}
\end{aligned}$

</details>

### Q248. MHT CET 2020 (12 Oct Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

Calculate the number of unit cells in $38.6 \mathrm{~g}$ of noble metal haven density $19 \cdot 3 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3}$ and volume of one unit cell is $6 \cdot 18 \times 10^{-23} \mathrm{~cm}^{3}$ ?

**Options:**

- **(A)** $3 \cdot 236 \times 10^{22}$
- **(B)** $6 \cdot 180 \times 10^{23}$
- **(C)** $6 \cdot 236 \times 10^{20}$
- **(D)** $3 \cdot 236 \times 10^{23}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) $3 \cdot 236 \times 10^{22}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

$\begin{aligned} \text { Vol. of metal }=\frac{\text { Mass }}{\text { Density }}=\frac{38.6 \mathrm{~g}}{19.3 \mathrm{~g} \mathrm{~cm}^{-3}} &=2 \mathrm{~cm}^{3} \\ \text { No. of unit cells in } 38.6 \mathrm{~g} \text { of noble metal } &=\frac{\text { Total vol. of metal }}{\text { Vol. of one unit cell }} \\ &=\frac{2 \mathrm{~cm}^{3}}{6.18 \times 10^{-23} \mathrm{~cm}^{3}}=3.236 \times 10^{22} \end{aligned}$

</details>

### Q249. MHT CET 2020 (12 Oct Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

What is the value of radius ratio of ionic crystal having coordination number six?

**Options:**

- **(A)** Greater than $0.732$
- **(B)** In between $0.414$ to $0.732$
- **(C)** In between $0 \cdot 225$ to $0 \cdot 414$
- **(D)** Less than $0.225$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) In between $0.414$ to $0.732$**

**Detailed Solution:**

Correct answer is in between $0.414$ and $0.732$
Since the general formula is $\mathrm{AX}$ with coordination number 6 , the crystal could be considered as $\mathrm{NaCl}$. Thus, it is evident that the ratio lies between $0.414$ and $0.732$ because of its octahedral structure.
The value ranging between $0.414$ and $0.732$ is obtained by dividing the radius of positive ion by that of negative ion.

</details>

### Q250. MHT CET 2020 (12 Oct Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Sodium crystallizes in bcc structure with radius $1 \cdot 86 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}$. What is the edge length of unit cell of sodium?

**Options:**

- **(A)** $4 \cdot 3 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}$
- **(B)** $3 \cdot 72 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}$
- **(C)** $7 \cdot 44 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}$
- **(D)** $5 \cdot 26 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) $4 \cdot 3 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

$\begin{aligned}
r &=1.86 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm} \\
a &=?
\end{aligned}$
For bcc structure, $r=\frac{\sqrt{3}}{4} a$
$\begin{array}{l}
\therefore \sqrt{3} \mathrm{a}=4 \mathrm{r} \\
\therefore a=\frac{4 \mathrm{r}}{\sqrt{3}}=\frac{4 \times 1.86 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}}{\sqrt{3}} \quad \therefore a=4.3 \times 10^{-8} \mathrm{~cm}
\end{array}$

</details>

### Q251. MHT CET 2019 (Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `3`

The percentage of unoccupied volume in simple cubic cell is

**Options:**

- **(A)** 52.40%
- **(B)** 32.00%
- **(C)** 47.60%
- **(D)** 68.04%

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) 47.60%**

**Detailed Solution:**

<strong>Key Idea</strong> Percentage of unoccupied volume = 100 – Packing efficiency Packing efficiency 
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mtext> </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mtext> </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mtext> </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mtext> </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mtext> </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mtext> </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mtext> </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mn>100</mn><mi>%</mi></math>  
For simple cubic cell, 
Packing efficiency <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>π</mi><msup><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn><msup><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mn>100</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>524</mn><mi>%</mi></math>  
<math><mo>∴</mo></math> Percentage of unoccupied volume in Scc = 100 – 52.4 = 47.6%

</details>

---

### Q252. MHT CET 2019 (Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

How many total constituent particles are present in simple cubic unit cell?

**Options:**

- **(A)** 1
- **(B)** 3
- **(C)** 4
- **(D)** 2

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) 1**

**Detailed Solution:**

A simple cubic lattice has only eight atoms on the corners. As the contribution by each <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>.</mo></math> therefore, number of constituent particles (atoms) present in a unit cell <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo></math>

</details>

### Q253. MHT CET 2019 (Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `3`

If a metal crystallises in bcc structure with edge length of unit cell <math><mn>4.29</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup></math> cm the radius of metal atom is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>3.2</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>1.86</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>1.07</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>1.07</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>1.86</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

The relation between edge length and radius of an atom in a bcc structure is related as: 
<math><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>/</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>α</mi></math>  
Given, <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4.29</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>  
For bcc structure, 
<math><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mn>4.29</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>  
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>1.857</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>≃</mo><mn>1.86</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>

</details>

### Q255. MHT CET 2016

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

In a face centred cubic unit cell, what is the volume occupied?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi> </mi><mi>π</mi><msup><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi> </mi><mi>π</mi><msup><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi> </mi><mi>π</mi><msup><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>64</mn><msup><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi> </mi><mi>π</mi><msup><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Number of atoms per unit cell in FCC <math> <mrow>  <mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></math>
So volume occupied by FCC unit cell
<math> <mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#x00D7;</mo><mfrac>  <mn>4</mn>  <mn>3</mn> </mfrac> <mi>&#x03C0;</mi><msup>  <mi>r</mi>  <mn>3</mn> </msup> </math>
<math> <mrow>  <mo>=</mo><mfrac>   <mrow>    <mn>16</mn></mrow>   <mn>3</mn>  </mfrac>  <mi>&#x03C0;</mi><msup>   <mi>r</mi>   <mn>3</mn>  </msup>  </mrow></math>

</details>

### Q256. MHT CET 2016

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Which metal crystallises in a simple cubic structure?

**Options:**

- **(A)** Polonium
- **(B)** Copper
- **(C)** Nickel
- **(D)** Iron

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) Polonium**

**Detailed Solution:**

Polonium is the only metal which crystallizes in a simple cubic structure.

</details>
